=== MITIGATION INVENTORY (Layer 3a / D4) — TEST FILES ===

Directive  : D4 — Layer 3a Mitigation Inventory (TEST split). DETERMINISTIC, read-only.
Scope      : TEST files only. Counterpart 'mitigation-inventory.txt' covers the
             complementary (non-test) set; together they partition first-party source.
Note       : No source files were modified to produce this artifact.

TEST-FILE SELECTION RULE (a repo-relative path is IN SCOPE iff it matches ANY of):
  - filename matches one of:  *.test.*  *.spec.*  *-test.*  *.e2e.*  *.test-suite.*  *.integration-test.*
  - OR the path contains a directory segment:  __tests__/   /test/   /tests/   /e2e/
  (Identical test-path rule to sink-inventory-test.txt.)

FILE EXTENSIONS SCANNED:  .ts  .tsx  .js  .jsx  .cjs  .mjs
exclude_dirs          :  node_modules  .yarn  .next  dist  build  .turbo  coverage  .git
TEST FILES IN SCOPE   :  900

METHODOLOGY: each in-scope test file is scanned with grep -InH --color=never -E <pattern>
for every category below. Matches are emitted as 'file:line:snippet' (snippet left-trimmed,
capped at 240 chars), de-duplicated, and sorted byte-deterministically (LC_ALL=C sort -u).
All ANSI escape sequences are stripped. A count of 0 is reported explicitly — NO category
is ever dropped (no silent failure). Output is byte-identical on identical repository input.

PATTERNS PER CATEGORY (extended regex, case-sensitive) — exactly as applied:
  [1] Input validation (Zod / class-validator)
      pattern: from "zod"|require\("zod"\)|\bz\.(object|string|number|enum|nativeEnum|array|union|discriminatedUnion|boolean|literal|coerce|date|record|tuple|map|set|bigint|any|unknown|optional|nullable|intersection|lazy|effect|preprocess)\b|\bsafeParse\b|\bZodSchema\b|\bZodError\b|\bZodType\b|class-validator|@(IsString|IsNotEmpty|IsEmail|IsNumber|IsBoolean|IsOptional|IsEnum|IsArray|IsInt|IsUUID|IsDate|IsUrl|IsObject|ValidateNested|Min|Max|Length|Matches|MinLength|MaxLength)\b|ValidationPipe
  [2] Parameterized query (Prisma)
      pattern: @prisma/client|\bPrismaClient\b|\bprisma\.|\bprismaMock\b|\bprismock\b|\$queryRaw\b|\$executeRaw\b
  [3] Authorization guards (UseGuards / PBAC / MembershipRole)
      pattern: @UseGuards|\bUseGuards\b|\bPBAC\b|\bMembershipRole\b|\bRolesGuard\b|\bAuthGuard\b|\bPermissionGuard\b|@Roles\b|\bcanAccess\b|\bcheckPermission\b|\bhasPermission\b|\bassertCanAccess\b|PermissionCheckService|\bResourcePermission|\bCrudAction\b
  [4] Authentication (NextAuth / Passport / verifyApiKey)
      pattern: next-auth|\bNextAuth\b|\bgetServerSession\b|\bgetSession\b|\buseSession\b|\bpassport\b|\bPassport\b|PassportStrategy|\bverifyApiKey\b|\bApiAuthGuard\b
  [5] Rate limiting (Throttler / rateLimit)
      pattern: \bThrottler\b|@Throttle\b|ThrottlerGuard|\brateLimit\b|rate-limit|\bRateLimit\b|\bcheckRateLimit\b|\brateLimiter\b|express-rate-limit
  [6] Crypto-correct (timingSafeEqual / bcrypt / GCM)
      pattern: \btimingSafeEqual\b|\bbcrypt\b|aes-256-gcm|aes-128-gcm|\bscrypt\b|\bargon2\b|\bpbkdf2\b
  [7] CSRF protection (csrfToken / sameSite)
      pattern: \bcsrfToken\b|\bcsrf\b|\bCSRF\b|\b_csrf\b|\bxsrf\b|\bsameSite\b|\bSameSite\b
  [8] Output sanitization (DOMPurify / escape)
      pattern: \bDOMPurify\b|\bdompurify\b|sanitize-html|\bsanitizeHtml\b|\bsanitize\b|\bescapeHtml\b|escape-html|\bescape\(|\bencodeURIComponent\b|\bxss\b
  [9] Security headers (helmet / CSP nonce)
      pattern: \bhelmet\b|Content-Security-Policy|\bcsp\b|\bnonce\b|X-Frame-Options|Strict-Transport-Security|X-Content-Type-Options|Referrer-Policy|Permissions-Policy
